            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement
                or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then tracked = i end
        end)
        UISvc.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if not on or i ~= tracked then return end
            local d = i.Position - from
            root.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(base.X.Offset + d.X, base.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end)
    end

    _G.MeerkoToggleInvisPanel = function()
        sg.Enabled = not sg.Enabled
        _G.MeerkoInvisShown = sg.Enabled
        if _G.MeerkoSaveSettings then pcall(_G.MeerkoSaveSettings) end
        return sg.Enabled
    end
end)

-- =====================================================================
--  AUTO KICK (порт "2DOHUB\auto kick or kick bind.txt").
--  Отдельное маленькое окно в ч/б палитре хаба, в нём две строки:
--    AUTO KICK -- тумблер. Пока включён, слушаем текст всех
--                TextLabel/TextButton/TextBox в PlayerGui и, как только
--                где-то появляется "you stole", вылетаем из сервера.
--                Состояние -- _G.MeerkoAutoKickOnSteal, пишется в
--                SideTP.json (ключ autoKickOnSteal, он там уже был).
--    KICK      -- бинд ручного кика: клик по клавише справа, затем любая
--                кнопка. Имя клавиши -- _G.MeerkoKickKeyName (ключ kickKey).
--                Пока бинд не задан, на плашке "NONE" и ничего не ловится.
--  Сам кик как в исходнике: сначала game:Shutdown(), если не прошёл --
--  LP:Kick(). Окно тянется за заголовок, позиция помнится в SideTP.json
--  (akX/akY). Скрыть/показать -- _G.MeerkoToggleAutoKick(), кикнуть из
--  кода -- _G.MeerkoKickNow().
-- =====================================================================
-- ============================================
-- UTILITY PANEL - Auto Kick + PS Code
-- Bỏ AJ, chỉ còn Kick + Private Server Code
-- ============================================

if not game:IsLoaded() then game.Loaded:Wait() end
task.wait(2)

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer
local PG = LP:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local UIS = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local HS = game:GetService("HttpService")
local guiParent = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") or PG

local C = {
    PANEL  = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 26),
    BTN    = Color3.fromRGB(36, 36, 46),
    BTN_ON = Color3.fromRGB(124, 92, 255),
    ACCENT = Color3.fromRGB(124, 92, 255),
    TEXT   = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 245),
    MUTED  = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 165),
    DANGER = Color3.fromRGB(230, 70, 90),
    STROKE = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 75),
    BG     = Color3.fromRGB(14, 14, 18),
}

local function corner(i, r)
    local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
    c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 8)
    c.Parent = i
    return c
end
local function stroke(i, col, t)
    local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
    s.Color = col or C.STROKE
    s.Thickness = t or 1
    s.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
    s.Parent = i
    return s
end

-- ===== XOÁ PANEL CŨ =====
pcall(function()
    local old = guiParent:FindFirstChild("UtilityUI")
    if old then old:Destroy() end
    local old2 = PG:FindFirstChild("UtilityUI")
    if old2 then old2:Destroy() end
end)

-- ===== CONFIG =====
local CFG_FILE = "UtilityUI.json"
local cfg = {}

local function loadCfg()
    if readfile then
        local ok, raw = pcall(readfile, CFG_FILE)
        if ok and type(raw) == "string" and #raw > 0 then
            local ok2, t = pcall(function() return HS:JSONDecode(raw) end)
            if ok2 and type(t) == "table" then cfg = t end
        end
    end
    return cfg
end

local function saveCfg()
    if not writefile then return end
    pcall(function()
        writefile(CFG_FILE, HS:JSONEncode(cfg))
    end)
end

loadCfg()

-- ===== STATE =====
_G.AutoKickOnSteal  = _G.AutoKickOnSteal or (cfg.akOn ~= nil and cfg.akOn or true)
_G.MynxxPrivateCode = _G.MynxxPrivateCode or cfg.psCode or ""
_G.AutoKickKeyword  = _G.AutoKickKeyword or "you stole"

-- ===== SCREEN GUI =====
local sg = Instance.new("ScreenGui")
sg.Name = "UtilityUI"
sg.ResetOnSpawn = false
sg.DisplayOrder = 999999
sg.IgnoreGuiInset = true
pcall(function() sg.Parent = guiParent end)
if not sg.Parent then sg.Parent = PG end

local MINI_H = 110
local FULL_H = 250

local posX = tonumber(cfg.x) or 300
local posY = tonumber(cfg.y) or 300

local f = Instance.new("Frame")
f.Name = "Panel"
f.Active = true
f.BorderSizePixel = 0
f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(210, MINI_H)
f.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(posX, posY)
f.BackgroundColor3 = C.PANEL
f.ClipsDescendants = false
f.ZIndex = 1
corner(f, 10)
stroke(f)
f.Parent = sg

local titleBar = Instance.new("TextButton", f)
titleBar.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28)
titleBar.BackgroundTransparency = 1
titleBar.Text = "UTILITY"
titleBar.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
titleBar.TextSize = 12
titleBar.TextColor3 = C.TEXT
titleBar.AutoButtonColor = false
titleBar.Active = true
titleBar.ZIndex = 3

local bar = Instance.new("Frame", f)
bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 2)
bar.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -14, 0, 26)
bar.BackgroundColor3 = C.ACCENT
bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
corner(bar, 2)
bar.ZIndex = 3

local body = Instance.new("Frame", f)
body.Name = "Body"
body.BackgroundTransparency = 1
body.BorderSizePixel = 0
body.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 32)
body.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, FULL_H - 32)
body.ZIndex = 2
body.ClipsDescendants = false

-- Kéo panel
do
    local dragging, dStart, pStart
    titleBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            dragging = true
            dStart = i.Position
            pStart = f.Position
        end
    end)
    titleBar.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            if dragging then
                cfg.x = f.Position.X.Offset
                cfg.y = f.Position.Y.Offset
                saveCfg()
            end
            dragging = false
        end
    end)
    UIS.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
        if not dragging then return end
        if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
            local d = i.Position - dStart
            f.Position = UDim2.new(pStart.X.Scale, pStart.X.Offset + d.X, pStart.Y.Scale, pStart.Y.Offset + d.Y)
        end
    end)
end

-- ===== AUTO KICK LOGIC =====
local fired = false
local function doKick()
    if fired then return end
    fired = true
    local code = _G.MynxxPrivateCode
    if type(code) == "string" and code ~= "" then
        task.delay(0.2, function()
            pcall(function()
                game:GetService("ExperienceService"):LaunchExperience({
                    placeId = game.PlaceId,
                    linkCode = code,
                })
            end)
        end)
        return
    end
    pcall(function() game:Shutdown() end)
    pcall(function() LP:Kick("\nAuto Kick") end)
end
_G.MynxxDoAutoKick = doKick

local function check(txt)
    if not _G.AutoKickOnSteal then return end
    if type(txt) ~= "string" or txt == "" then return end
    local kw = tostring(_G.AutoKickKeyword or "you stole")
    if string.find(string.lower(txt), kw, 1, true) then doKick() end
end

local hooked = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" })
local function hookObj(obj)
    if hooked[obj] then return end
    hooked[obj] = true
    check(obj.Text)
    obj:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function() check(obj.Text) end)
end
local function isText(o)
    return o:IsA("TextLabel") or o:IsA("TextButton") or o:IsA("TextBox")
end
local function watchRoot(root)
    for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
        if isText(obj) then hookObj(obj) end
    end
    root.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(desc)
        if isText(desc) then hookObj(desc) end
    end)
end
for _, g in ipairs(PG:GetChildren()) do pcall(watchRoot, g) end
PG.ChildAdded:Connect(function(g) pcall(watchRoot, g) end)

-- ===== HELPERS =====
local function mkButton(x, y, w, h, text, onClick, accent, parent)
    local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
    b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(w, h)
    b.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(x, y)
    b.BackgroundColor3 = accent and C.ACCENT or C.BTN
    b.Text = text
    b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    b.TextSize = 11
    b.TextColor3 = C.TEXT
    b.BorderSizePixel = 0
    b.AutoButtonColor = false
    b.ZIndex = 10
    b.Active = true
    b.Selectable = true
    corner(b, 7)
    b.Parent = parent or body
    if onClick then b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(onClick) end
    return b
end

-- ===== ROW 1: AUTO KICK =====
local akBtn = mkButton(8, 0, 152, 26, "", function() end)
local function refreshAK()
    akBtn.Text = "AUTO KICK" .. (_G.AutoKickOnSteal and ": ON" or ": OFF")
    akBtn.BackgroundColor3 = _G.AutoKickOnSteal and C.BTN_ON or C.BTN
end
akBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    _G.AutoKickOnSteal = not _G.AutoKickOnSteal
    cfg.akOn = _G.AutoKickOnSteal
    saveCfg()
    refreshAK()
end)
refreshAK()

-- ===== ROW 2: KICK (to private server nếu có PS code) =====
local kbBtn = mkButton(8, 30, 194, 26, "KICK", function()
    local code = _G.MynxxPrivateCode
    if type(code) == "string" and code ~= "" then
        -- Vào private server
        kbBtn.Text = "JOINING PS..."
        task.delay(0.2, function()
            local ok = pcall(function()
                game:GetService("ExperienceService"):LaunchExperience({
                    placeId = game.PlaceId,
                    linkCode = code,
                })
            end)
            if not ok then
                -- Fallback: kick thẳng nếu LaunchExperience fail
                pcall(function() game:Shutdown() end)
                pcall(function() LP:Kick("\nUtility") end)
            end
        end)
    else
        -- Không có PS code → kick thẳng
        pcall(function() game:Shutdown() end)
        pcall(function() LP:Kick("\nUtility") end)
    end
end)
kbBtn.TextColor3 = C.DANGER
kbBtn.BackgroundColor3 = C.BG

    else
        tb.TextTransparency = 0
    end

    reveal = not reveal
    eye.BackgroundColor3 = reveal and C.BTN_ON or C.BTN
    eye.Text = reveal and "HIDE" or "SHOW"
    tb.TextTransparency = (not reveal and #tb.Text > 0) and 0.7 or 0
end)

    _G.MynxxPrivateCode = tb.Text
    cfg.psCode = tb.Text
    saveCfg()
end)

-- ===== NÚT EXPAND =====
local expandBtn = mkButton(164, 0, 38, 26, "*", function() end)

local expanded = cfg.expanded == true

local function setExpanded(state)
    expanded = state
    if expanded then
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(210, FULL_H)
        ext.Visible = true
        expandBtn.Text = "-"
    else
        f.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(210, MINI_H)
        ext.Visible = false
        expandBtn.Text = "*"
    end
    cfg.expanded = expanded
    saveCfg()
end

expandBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    setExpanded(not expanded)
end)

if expanded then
    task.defer(function() setExpanded(true) end)
end

print("[Utility] Loaded! Pos: " .. posX .. "," .. posY .. " | Expanded: " .. tostring(expanded))
print("[Utility] PS code: " .. (tostring(_G.MynxxPrivateCode ~= "" and "yes" or "no")))

-- =====================================================================
--  КНОПКА-СКРЫВАЛКА ПАНЕЛЕЙ -- узкая плашка справа по середине экрана.
--  Один клик убирает с экрана три окна сразу: EXTRAS, TP SPEED и AUTO KICK
--  (ScreenGui MeerkoExtras / MeerkoTPSpeed / MeerkoAutoKick), второй возвращает.
--  STEAL TARGET, INVIS и PR LIST не трогает -- у них свои кнопки.
--  Возвращает ровно то, что было видно на момент скрытия: перед тем как гасить,
--  снимаем слепок Enabled по каждому окну. Закрыл AUTO KICK своей кнопкой, потом
--  дёрнул скрывалку туда-обратно -- AUTO KICK останется закрытым, а не выскочит.
--  Состояние помнится в SideTP.json (поле panelsHidden): вошёл в игру с убранными
--  панелями -- они и останутся убранными, сама кнопка при этом всегда на экране.
--  Ручки: _G.MeerkoTogglePanelsHidden(), _G.MeerkoSetPanelsHidden(true/false),
--         _G.MeerkoPanelsHidden -- состояние.
-- =====================================================================
if _G.MeerkoPanelsHidden == nil then _G.MeerkoPanelsHidden = false end
_G.MeerkoLate("PANEL HIDE", function()
    local C = {
        bg   = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 10),
        bg2  = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 18),
        rowH = Color3.fromRGB(44, 44, 44),
        acc  = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 235),
        acc2 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        txt  = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255),
        dim  = Color3.fromRGB(140, 140, 140),
    }
    local FB = Enum.Font.GothamBold
    local TS = game:GetService("TweenService")
    local EASE = TweenInfo.new(0.16, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
    local function tw(o, p) TS:Create(o, EASE, p):Play() end
    local function mk(cls, parent, props)
        local o = Instance.new(cls)
        for k, v in pairs(props or {}) do o[k] = v end
        o.Parent = parent
        return o
    end
    local function round(o, r) mk("UICorner", o, { CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, r or 8) }) return o end

    local host = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui")
    pcall(function()
        local old = host:FindFirstChild("MeerkoPanelHide")
        if old then old:Destroy() end
    end)
    -- DisplayOrder выше всех окон хаба: кнопка должна быть кликабельной всегда
    local sg = mk("ScreenGui", nil, {
        Name = "MeerkoPanelHide", ResetOnSpawn = false,
        IgnoreGuiInset = true, DisplayOrder = 1000000,
        ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling,
    })
    pcall(function() sg.Parent = host end)
    if not sg.Parent then
        pcall(function() sg.Parent = (gethui and gethui()) or game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
    end
    -- какие окна гасим; snap -- слепок Enabled на момент скрытия
    local PANELS = { "MeerkoExtras", "MeerkoTPSpeed", "MeerkoAutoKick" }
    local snap = {}
    local function findPanel(name)
        local g = host and host:FindFirstChild(name)
        if g then return g end
        -- если gethui() не сработал, окно могло собраться прямо в CoreGui
        local ok, cg = pcall(function() return game:GetService("CoreGui") end)
        if ok and cg and cg ~= host then return cg:FindFirstChild(name) end
        return nil
    end
    -- чем считать окно, если слепка нет (вошли уже с panelsHidden = true):
    -- EXTRAS помнит себя сам полем extrasShown, остальные два по умолчанию видимы
    local function defaultShown(name)
        if name == "MeerkoExtras" then return _G.MeerkoExtrasShown ~= false end
        return true
    end
    local function applyHidden(hidden, remember)
        for _, n in ipairs(PANELS) do
            local g = findPanel(n)
            if g then
                if hidden then
                    if remember then snap[n] = g.Enabled and true or false end
                    g.Enabled = false
                else
                    local was = snap[n]
                    if was == nil then was = defaultShown(n) end
                    g.Enabled = was and true or false
                end
            end
        end
        if not hidden then snap = {} end
    end
    -- Сама плашка: узкая, прижата к правому краю и отцентрована по высоте
    -- (AnchorPoint 1,0.5 + Position 1,-8 / 0.5,0 -- держится на любом экране).
    local card = round(mk("TextButton", sg, {
        Name = "Tab", AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5),
        Position = UDim2.new(1, -6, 0.5, 0), Size = UDim2.fromOffset(22, 56),
        BackgroundColor3 = C.bg, BorderSizePixel = 0,
        Text = "", AutoButtonColor = false, Active = true,
    }), 10)
    mk("UIGradient", card, {
        Rotation = 135,
        Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, C.bg2), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, C.bg)}),
    })
    local rim = mk("UIStroke", card, { Thickness = 1.1, Transparency = 0.3, Color = C.acc })
    mk("UIGradient", rim, {
        Rotation = 135,
        Color = ColorSequence.new({ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, C.acc), ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, C.acc2)}),
    })
    round(mk("Frame", card, {
        Size = UDim2.new(1, 6, 1, 6), Position = UDim2.fromOffset(-4, -4),
        BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = 0.8,
        ZIndex = -1, BorderSizePixel = 0,
    }), 12)
    local tag = mk("TextLabel", card, {
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 8), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 9),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = FB, TextSize = 7,
        TextColor3 = C.acc, Text = "UI", ZIndex = 2,
    })
    -- шеврон смотрит в сторону действия: ">>" -- убрать вправо, "<<" -- вернуть
    local chev = mk("TextLabel", card, {
        Position = UDim2.fromOffset(0, 20), Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28),
        BackgroundTransparency = 1, Font = FB, TextSize = 14,
        TextColor3 = C.txt, Text = ">>", ZIndex = 2,
    })
    local function paint()
        local hidden = _G.MeerkoPanelsHidden == true
        chev.Text = hidden and "<<" or ">>"
        tw(chev, { TextColor3 = hidden and C.dim or C.txt })
        tw(tag,  { TextColor3 = hidden and C.dim or C.acc })
    end
    local function setHidden(v)
        v = v and true or false
        _G.MeerkoPanelsHidden = v
        applyHidden(v, true)
        paint()
        if _G.MeerkoSaveSettings then pcall(_G.MeerkoSaveSettings) end
        return v
    end

    card.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        setHidden(not (_G.MeerkoPanelsHidden == true))
    end)
    card.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tw(card, { BackgroundColor3 = C.rowH }) end)
    card.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tw(card, { BackgroundColor3 = C.bg }) end)

    -- Вошли с убранными панелями -- гасим их сразу. Слепок при этом не снимаем:
    -- показ вернёт EXTRAS по его собственному extrasShown, а TP SPEED и AUTO KICK
    -- по умолчанию видимы. Блок стоит последним в очереди MeerkoLate, так что все
    -- три окна к этому моменту уже собраны.
    if _G.MeerkoPanelsHidden == true then applyHidden(true, false) end
    paint()

    _G.MeerkoTogglePanelsHidden = function()
        return setHidden(not (_G.MeerkoPanelsHidden == true))
    end
    _G.MeerkoSetPanelsHidden = function(v) return setHidden(v) end
end)




-- LOL OPTIZ: red name ESP for brainrots in the current PRIO list.
;(function()
    if type(_G.LolOptizStopPrioESP) == "function" then pcall(_G.LolOptizStopPrioESP) end
    local running = true
    local entries = {}
    local function remove(key)
        local entry = entries[key]
        if entry then
            entry.gui:Destroy()
            entry.anchor:Destroy()
            entries[key] = nil
        end
    end
    _G.LolOptizStopPrioESP = function()
        running = false
        for key in pairs(entries) do remove(key) end
    end
    local function normalize(name)
        return tostring(name):lower():gsub("[%s%-_'%.]", "")
    end
    task.spawn(function()
        local player = game:GetService("Players").LocalPlayer
        local host = player:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
        while running do
            pcall(function()
                local scan = _G.MeerkoScanCached
                if type(scan) ~= "function" then return end
                local pets = scan(0.5)
                if not running then return end
                if type(pets) ~= "table" then
                    for key in pairs(entries) do remove(key) end
                    return
                end
                local priority = {}
                for _, name in ipairs(_G.SHARED_PRIORITY_ITEMS or {}) do
                    priority[normalize(name)] = true
                end
                local seen = {}
                for _, pet in ipairs(pets) do
                    if priority[normalize(pet.name)] or (pet.index and priority[normalize(pet.index)]) then
                        local pos = pet.position
                        if pet.conveyor and _G.MeerkoConveyorPos then pos = _G.MeerkoConveyorPos(pet) end
                        if typeof(pos) == "Vector3" then
                            local key = pet.model or (tostring(pet.plot) .. ":" .. tostring(pet.slot))
                            local entry = entries[key]
                            if entry and not entry.gui.Parent then remove(key); entry = nil end
                            if not entry then
                                local anchor = Instance.new("Attachment")
                                anchor.Name = "LolOptizPrioESPAnchor"
                                anchor.Parent = workspace.Terrain
                                local gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
                                gui.Name = "LolOptizPrioESP"
                                gui.Adornee = anchor
                                gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
                                gui.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(210, 28)
                                gui.StudsOffsetWorldSpace = Vector3.new(0, 4, 0)
                                gui.ResetOnSpawn = false
                                gui.LightInfluence = 0
                                gui.Parent = host
                                local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
                                label.Size = UDim2.fromScale(1, 1)
                                label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                                label.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)
                                label.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
                                label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.2
                                label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
                                label.TextSize = 12
                                label.TextWrapped = true
                                label.Parent = gui
                                entry = { anchor = anchor, gui = gui, label = label }
                                entries[key] = entry
                            end
                            entry.anchor.WorldPosition = pos
                            entry.label.Text = tostring(pet.name or pet.index)
                            seen[key] = true
                        end
                    end
                end
                for key in pairs(entries) do
                    if not seen[key] then remove(key) end
                end
            end)
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end)()
